41b3(cha): mngon gsum gnad gzir bas//ma bcos bzhi la gnas pa'o//rkyen gyis gnas pa 'byung ba yi//lus kyi bye brag dag pa dang //ma dag pa'i khyad par gyis//ye shes gnas par byed pas ni//so so'i dus tshod mkhas pas bzung //
41b4(cha): rgyu yis gnas pas seMn ni//kun la khyab phyir chos nyid kyang //bcos pa med na gnas pa yin//bar skabs gnas pa rlung gi grangs//'gyu ba 'phral ba'i bar du shes//'dis ni ye shes gnas 'dus pa'o//de las 'khrul pa rgyu dang
41b5(cha): rkyen tha che dang yan lag las nyid dang //ma smin rnam rtog 'khor ba'o//ma rig pa ni rnam pa gsum//bdag nyid cig pas 'khrul rtsa byas//lhan cig skyes pas rtog pa gnyis//kun tu brtags pas yul du gyur//rkyen ni rnam pa bzhi dag
41b6(cha): gis / 'du shes bzung bar 'khrul ga'i byas//cha ni 'du byed la sogs pa//'a khrul yul cha las bcu gnyis bri/smin pas lo dang byed laso//yan lag spros pa tha dad las//wang ming gi bye brag tu//'khrul pa'i yul la rnam gnyis 'gyur//las
41b7(cha): kyi 'dod dang gzugs kyi khams//gzugs med rnams te rnam gsum byed//ma smin pas ni rigs drug gzugs//ming dang gzugs bye brag tha dad 'gyur//rnam rtog bag la nyal ba dang //'a khrul 'apa'i u la rnam gnyis 'gyur na/las gyi 'dod dang gzugs
42a1(cha): @#kun nas khyab dang ldang chu'o//'khor ba zhes pa mtshungs pa dang //'tshogs pa'i lam dang sdud mched de//rdzogs dang bye brag sna tshogs pa'o//de ltar la khrul zhes ming tsam las//rtog 'dzin med pa'i rtags dang ni//'tshogs shing
42a2(cha): sdud pa'i byed pa can//ming gi bye brag gis btag so//'khrul pa'i rgyu ni ma rig pa//gzhi dang shes pa spags pa dang //gzung dang dbang po 'khor tshu lo//rkyen ni yul dang bzung cha la//mtha' yi mtha' yis 'byed pa dang //cig ma shes pa'i dmigs/
42a3(cha): rten no//gzhi ni gar yang 'byed med cing //nges pa med pa tsam la so//shes pa 'gyur dang yal ba dang //mched par 'dzin pa rnams yino//sbags pa dri mar 'dzin pa ste//shes byas rang rgyud bcings pa'o//gzung ba ma mchis pa la yang //bden pa
42a4(cha): dam du bcings pa'o//dbang po'i yul dang shes pa'i chas//rtags 'dzin yul la der snang ba'o//'khor tshul cig par shes pa ni//yul rtog gnyis gnyis zung 'jug/mtha'i byed pa las dang dgu//'byung ba chen po'i lasu 'gyur//cig ma
42a5(cha): shes pa'i laT ka dag las//chos nyid rang ngo ma shes pas//rgyu dang mtshungs pa nyer len no//dmigs pa'i rten ni kha dog las//gnyis cha 'phra ba'i 'gyu rten gyis//'khor ba las kyi dmigs 'gyu ro//mtshan nyid rtags pa'i bye brag ni//yod dang
42a6(cha): med dang snang ba dang //gsal dang stong dang rags pa 'dzin//rnam shes 'dzin pa'i gzugs can no//yod pa dngos po'i byed pa can//snang dang mtshan nyid 'jug pas khyab//las dang bye brag nye bar ston//med pa gzugs la sogs pa spangs//yid dang
42a7(cha): yid dang shes pa 'jug pa can//mtshan nyid rtag tu mi rnyed pa'o//snang ba gzugs la sogs pa'o//'dzin cing mched pa sdud par byed//gnyis 'jug khyab pa'i rang bzhin can//gsal bas rang ngo ston pa dang //mi snang ba las log pa'o//stong pa skye/
42b1(cha): ba ma yin la//gangs dang mtha' dbus med pa ston//ma byung bas na 'gag med do///rags pa rdul dang phra rab la//sna tshogs gzug su rdzogs pa dang //reg yod 'jig par snang ba'o//'dzin pa dbang po'i yul dag las//so sos
42b2(cha): bzung bas 'ching ba dang //bdag dang nyon mongs las kun byed//rnam par shes pa rig pa'i na//yul la 'jug cing bcad pa dang //rtag dang las kyi rnar rtog/gzugs can rigs can bems po ste//sems sdud pa dang bkod pa'o//son kun
42b3(cha): gyi tha dad rgyu//'byung ba'i rgyu dang byed las dang //dbang po nyid dang dbyibs dang tshad//sgra dang dngos po brjod pa dang //gnas pas snod kyi bye brago//'byung ba'i rgyu ni gzhi dag las//btags pas bcu drug dag yin te//rnam par phye bas bzhi
42b4(cha): brgya'o bying ba'i las ni gnyis dag las//btags pas bcu gnyis dag tu 'dod//de la rigs drug tha dad do//dbang po'i mig la sogs pa'o//dbyibs dang kha dog mi 'thun pa//bzhi brgya dag dang bzhi yis kyang //son lus kyang tha dad do//
42b5(cha): dbyibs ni lus dang yan lag dang //so so'i gling dang dbang pos kyang //'gro ba'i gzugs kyang des phye'o//tshad ni rigs drug so so ste//thog mi bar dang tha ma dang //so so'i bsod nams las kyis dbye//sgra ni ring dang thung ba dang //drag
42b6(cha): zhan dang ni gzir dang g.yung //'phra dang sing dang rtse mo'o//dngos po sa chu me rlung gis//shes pa nyid dang bsod nams dang //dbang thang lus kyi gshed kyis brtag/brjod pa gnas dang 'byung ba dang //las dang rtag pa rang 'dod do//gnas pa'i
42b7(cha): snod ni dag pa dang //ma dag pa yang bcud kyis dbye//de nas 'byung ba'i rang gnad las//seMn lus kyang tha dad do//rang bzhin rdzogs pa'i gnas skabs ni//chos nyid dbyings dang rang ngo dang //'gyu ba stongs dang rtog pa zad//bzhi rdzogs lam dang 'bras
43a1(cha): @#khu'o/ chos nyid sus kyang bcos pa med tsa ma/rang bzhin dag las spros pa bral//rnam rtog tha dang yul las 'das//tshig 'byung la khrul pa rgyun 'chad pa'o//dbyings ni phyi dang nang du ste//byi ni sprin bral mkha' la 'dzin//bag rdul
43a2(cha): char gyis dengs dus su//mkhas pas mngon gsum gnad gzir dang /nang gi sgron ma lam du bya'o//rang ngo rig pa lu gu rgyud//'gro 'o bral te mkha' la'o//'gyu ba rlung gnyis byed pa bcad//dgu pa yis ni rlung gis dbye//gnas kyi 'jug pa thabs kyi
43a3(cha): bya'o//res ni rlung dag stongs pa yis//snang ba thad zad par 'gyur//de ltar rnam rtog zad pa dang //skye med ye shes dus mnyam ste//'di tshe 'khor 'das 'dres pa'o//de yang gzhi las rdzogs pa'i gnang //rang bzhin ma bcos mngon du snang //lam ni/
43a4(cha): sgron mas 'tshams sbyar nas//mngon gsum nyid du snang ba'o//'bras bu gang du 'dod pa med//'dod dang ri dogs yul las 'das//rang snang ka nas dag pa rdzogs//shes 'dzin rtag pa kun zad pa'o//ming gi mchog nyid byed pa ni//rgyu dang rkyen dang brtags
43a5(cha): pa dang /mtha' dang dang po tha mas bsgyur//btags pas ma rnyed tha snyad gzhi//ming gi rgyu ni tshig gi mtha'//mtshan nyid gang du yang ma grub pas/bltar snang bdag tu med pa'o//rkyen ni dbang po thad ka'i yul//so so'i snang zhe na bcas pa la//
43a6(cha): kun gzhi 'tshogs po'i snang char shar//brtags pas brda dang bsgyur byed pas//mtshan nyid nye bar ston pa dang //khyad par ming gis gang bsgyur gnang //so so'i skabs kyang sbyar byas nas//bac+doMs kyang 'jig par 'gyur//mtha' ni 'byung ba'i gnad kyis
43a7(cha): gyes pas dang bso dang 'jig pa'i las/ming nga rten rnying 'brel bas bya'o//dang po gang gra ba'i ming po ge dang bongs la led pa yis/pa di nga 'a'adang gnas pazhi pa/so ra'i tshang gyis shes par bya'o//maba'ams bsgyur ba ming yi ma bal/'azlog cing 'bus/
43b1(cha): pas 'byung lu sa sbyar//yang dag chos nyid rtog bral la//ming gi mtshan nyid rab phye nas//phyi dang nang la rab brtags phra//ming gi mtshan nyid ma rnyed pas//ngag gi cho ga rdzogs par 'dod//tha snyad rnams ni tshig gi mtha'/gang du yod med mtha' yis dbye//
43b2(cha): lus sems gnyis las brtags bshig pas//tha snyad ba'i yang stongs pa'o//gzhi ni gcig dang gnyi su min//stags stongs yod med 'gag pa ste//byas dang byung 'a dus mi rnyed//de bzhin gnas dang 'gro sa'ang med//med phyir chos nyid stong pa'o//
43b3(cha): sen las dang dbang po ni//rlung dang shes pa spyi snod dang //'byung ba spyi dang khyab byed tshul//gsum dang dgu dang nyer gcig/sen las ni rlung gis yang ka 'byung ba 'phen pa dag tu 'phul//de la las kyi bye brag ni//'jug cing thabs kyis rab tu 'byed//
43b4(cha): de'i byed pas shes pa ni//mkha' la sprin gyi rang bzhin nyid//rlung gis gtor ba ji bzhin du//dum bu dum bus las snang ston//de las lus ngag yid dag las//smin pas las kyi rnam par ston//'byung bas sa chu me rlung gi/lus su rdzogs pas rlung
43b5(cha): gi las//khyab byed son thang la//'byung bzhi'i lus las ma 'das pas//so so'i las kyi sdib su'o//tshul ni gzug su grub pa dang //de'i las kyi snga phyi la//so sor blangs te grub par 'gyur//dbang po gsum ni rab 'bring tha//des ni theg gsum
43b6(cha): 'du shes bral//de las gsum gsum rab phye bas//dgu ste theg dgu dbang po'i gnang //de las gnyisu rab phye ste//dgu phrag gnyis te snang ba'i cha//bzhi la gsum bzhag nyer gcig gis//snang ba nyer gcig rdzogs pa'o//tshig gi 'jug pa
43b7(cha): 'di lta ste//tshangs pa brgya byin khyab 'jug dang //mkha' lding dbang phyug drag po dang //so so'i sgra dang lha yi skad//gnas gzhan brtsis pas tshig la 'jug/tshangs pa tshig bshad dang po dang //phyed rdzogs gnas gzhan dag tu 'gyur//dbang
44a1(cha): @zlog gis ni tshig bshad dgu//de'i gnas kyi phyed gnyis 'gyur//khyab 'jug gis ni stong gnyis las//gnas la dbang bas gcig rdzog so//brgya byin gyis ni tshigs bcad lnga//sgra yis bzlog pas sum gnyis
44a2(cha): rdzogs 'a mkha' lding tshigs bcad drug gis yang //rtags phye bas ni bdun gnyis la'o//drag po'i 'jug pa sum cu la//rtags bkod las kyi phyag rgya bzhi'o//so so'i sgra ni gnas pa dang //'jug dang 'byung dang bzlog pa las//dus la brten pa blo
44a3(cha): ldan rdzogs//lha'i skad ni stong phrag drug/yan lag spras pa 'di gnyis 'gyur//gnas gzhan brtsis pa bzhi dag la//tshig la rtogs pa gnas brgyad gnyis//de las spros pa bzhi brgya bzhi//khyad par nges pas bcur 'gyuraro//de dag rab tu
44a4(cha): sbyor shes pas//tshig la dbang ste smra ba'i rgyal//des ni sbyor mchog nges grub bo//blo dang shes pa shes bya ba/sems dang yid dang 'gyu ba dang //'phro dang rgod dang rengs pa dang //yul dang 'dzin pa zhen pa'o//sems ni dag dang ma dag las//
44a5(cha): khams gsum po yang sangs rgyas 'jug/yid ni ngo bo nges tshig dang //dbye ba dag gis shes par 'gyur/'gyu ba dran dang snang ba'i cha//brtags pa las ni blo 'gyuraro//'phro ba yul gyi las mtha' las//blo dang shes pa'i sprul du snang //rgod pa
44a6(cha): rlung gis 'byed pa'i las//'du byed rlung dang shes pa'i rta'o//rengs pa nyid dang gsum dag las//blo zhes 'gyus dang shes pas gcod//gnyis 'dus pa las khams gsum byung //yul ni sgo lnga'i snang bya las//blo dang shes pa 'byung ba byung //
44a7(cha): 'dzin pa mig la sogs pa yis//de dang de la blo zhes dngos//zhen pa'i blo'i rtog tshogs bzhi//shes pa'i tshad las brgyad du 'gyur//de las 'byung ba 'jig chag so//de ltar snang cha'i byed pa'i las//gzung dang 'dzin pa gnyi su shar//gzung ba yul las
44b1(cha): 'dzin pa sems//lus sems 'dus pas dum gsu so//lus gnang ngag la dag gnang sams//sems gdang mig la rog gnang rtsa//des na lus kyi 'dug sbyangs las//dag chags lus kyi 'brel chod dang //sangs rgyas sos parjo
44b2(cha): pa'o rtsa ni rten 'brel 'khor lo yis//bsku la ming bzung dang sbrel ba'i gnang //rna khyor pa'i yan lago//ngo bo ka dag ye shes la//ma rig pa zhes srid ming med//gcig dang gnyis kyi grangs med pa'o//brtags pa yod med grub pa
44b3(cha): med/gang du ma phye chos nyid las//ye shes tsam du grub pa med//tshig med brjod pas grub pa med//mtha' la mi gnas rang rig dag/brjod 'dzin ming gi mtha' zang ba'o//sgyu med rkyen gyi grangs med la//gnyis snang yul dang yul can ming
44b4(cha): mtshan nyid gang du zla ba med//yul rkyen rags pa rang za pa'o//se ras med pas dag pas dag/bgrub pa 'gags pas cir mi ldang ba/ma skyes pas na 'bab stong ngo //rang bzhin lhur grub yi shes zhes//mi skyo mi gag tsor mi dgongs//yul
44b5(cha): dag nges pa med phyir ro//rtsal dang ston 'gag med pas//rol pa stsal du snang ba las//sna tshogs rdzogs pa'i gzhi ma'o//med phyir snang la snong phyir stong //snang stong 'jug pa'i yan lag las//sangs rgyas son ngag yulo//'di ltar bzhi zhes mngon
44b6(cha): par gnas//rang bzhin tsam du gnas pa las//ngo bo'i cha nas btags pas stong //yan lag spro phyir mkhyen pa rdzogs//rang gnas lhun gyis grub pa'i phyir//'di la spang blang gnyis med pa'o//thugs rjes kun khyab ye shes las//mi mdzad sna
44b7(cha): tshogs shar ba'i sgo//mdzad pa ltar snang ngo bor rdzogs//chos sku stong pa'i rang bzhin las//ye shes mkhyen pa rdzogs pa'i cha//dbugs kyis seMn rnams la bachad//de med 'khor 'das gangos chad pas//mkhyen pas rig cing gsal ba'o//rang rig
45a1(cha): gsal ba'i bdag nyid las//rang bzhin shugs kyi thugs rje nyid//ma 'gags 'gag pa med pa'o//'byung ba dag pa'i cha nas ni//las su med la cig rdzogs pa'o//nyi ma las ni 'od bzhin du//rang bcas sgrib pa
45a2(cha): med pa'o//de las mtshan nyid 'dzin pa'o//longs sku'i ye shes bA shad pa'o//me long ye shes gzugs brnyan gsal//dbyibs dang kha dog snang cha rdzogs//dkar po'i dri ma dag phyiraro//rab chas snang ste 'khor 'angs sbyor//chos rnams kun/
45a3(cha): gyi gzugs mthong bas//rang rig ye shes zhes bya'o//gsal zhing stong la stong pas grol//grol 'dzin mtha'i rtog pa zad //bzhag med grol bzhi rdzogs pa'o//mnyam pa nyid ces bya ba ni//mnyam rgyud nyid la mnyam rkyen gsum//
45a4(cha): mnyam pa'i dus dang tshad kyi so//'di la gnyis med rigs dang bral//ma phye mi phyed rang gnas nyid//mnyam gnas zhes bya chos nyid la//zug pu spros bral ba'o//nyid ces bcos shing rtsol sgrub med//rang bzhin gnas pas ngo bo stong //sem
45a5(cha): nyid rtog pa kun zad pa'o//ye shes zhes bya gnas pa la//de'i mtshan nyid rdzogs pa yi//'khor 'das gnyis las mi gnas pa'o//so sor rtog pa dbang po'i rigs//gang la gang snang de'i chos//rig pa so sor gsal ba'o//brtags pas
45a6(cha): rang snang dag nas ni//rig pa yul du gyur pa nyid/so sang shes pa'i bye brag las/gnyen po'i tshul du byung bya ste//rtogs pa zhes ni mtshan nyid mthong /rang snang 'phel dang bcas pa'o//ye shes thog mar byung ba la/
45a7(cha): shes pa nyon mongs rkyen dag go/bya'i grub pa zhes bya la//'bad cing rtsol ba rang 'gags nas//chos kun rang bzhag rang grol lo//rang grol rdzogs pa'i gni snang wa/cha pra rdul phran dri med thob//rtog dpyod yul las 'das pa ste//gang /
45b1(cha): gis ma bye chas nyid do//rang bzhin rdzogs pas snang mtha' zang / cig car rtogs pas brubab tu grub pas yod ba bral ba la/sbyar dang bzlog pa med pa la/lenas gnas pa'i ngan nyid la//shes pas mngon du gyur nas mi//chos rnams zang yin

46a1(cha): ye shes bshad//snang chos brjod gzhi bral ba la//rang snang ba'i gzugs dag la//dngos po'i gnas lugs mkhyen pa la//rang don rtogs pas 'khrul rgyun zad//ji zhes bya ba gnas lugs la//lta ba yengs pa med par

46a7(cha): 'dra ba 'a 'di las byung ba'i nyams myong ni//gang gis brjod par mi nus pa'i//e ma ngo mtshar che ba nyid//ston rdzogs pa'i snang ba'o//khyab phye cha 'phra ye shes las//mngon gsum pa dang lam du'o//gzhan yang rab tu bshad par bya//rnam rnyog 'dus pa'i/

52b7(cha): sams pas yid dpyod ngo do//don ni rang snang rang gis lha//mngon gsum tshad ni gsum dag gis//lu gu rgyud la mngon du spyod//gcer mthang ba dang gcer grol du//gang gis mthong ba'i don yom//lta ba'i ngo bo mthong ba la//yang dbyang tshig gis/
52'a1(cha): 'gyus pa med//lung dang bla ma'i man ngag las//gang gis bsten pa med pa'o//lta ba'i rang bzhin snod bcud la//ma khyab med pas gang yang grol//grol ba'i chos la rnam smin bral//cir snang rang gi chos nyid las//bcos ma'i chos
52'a2(cha): nyid logs na med//ji ltar spyad kyang me tog skye/spang dang blang ba rang snang ngo //brjod tshig ming gi rnam grangs ni//phyi dang nang dang gsang ba dang //bla na med pa'i lta ba bzhi'o//phyi ni chos can la lta ba//rim brgyad so so'i 'dod pa la//yid dpyod 'dzin pa nyid du
52'a3(cha): 'dod//nang gi chos nyid la bltas pas//ngo dang rang bzhin sgyu mar mthong //gsang ba rang rig la bltas pas//lu gu rgyud du 'brel chags mthong //kho na nyid ni dbyings rig go//bsgom pa zhes ni sems gnas pa//'gyu ba phyi nang gcod pa ste//gzung dang
52'a4(cha): 'dzin pa 'gag pa'o//rang bzhin 'bas las byung ba ni//bsam gtan dag tu brjod par bya//rlung dang rtsa dang thig le dang //lus gnang dmigs dang stong pa ni//bsgom pa zhes ni 'dod pa yin//yi ge'i 'od deng kha dog dang //rnar 'phrul pa'i spro bsdu ni//ting
52'a5(cha): 'dzin zhes bshad pa la//ma bcos rang bzhag gnas lugs te//ji ltar de las 'gyur med pa///sangs rgyas rnams kyi dgongs pa'o//de'i las dang gol sa ni//las kyi yul zhen gcod pa dang //nyon mongs la sogs spong ba dang //'khor ba'i rtsa ba
52'a6(cha): /'byin pa'o//go ba sems nyid 'dzin pa dang //gnas par byed dang rlung zad dang //chos nyid stong pa'i lam rnyed dang //rang bzhin dag pa'i gzhi snang ngo //ma gol gnas pa'i sgom pa ni//dbang po'i sgo rnams bsal ba las//yid kyi rtog pa ma 'gyus pas//shre/
52'a7(cha): ba nyid la spyod pa'i yig phyir la shor ba med pa nyid//rigs can gsal pam yin dang //lung bcang stong bar ma song dang //'de ba rkyen chags ma yin dang //btang snyoms sbrengs por ma song dang //dran rig gsal ba ngal so dang //sbyangs pa
52'b1(cha): log char las song dang //'dzin mchang du rgyun bde dang //cha lugs gsal ba 'dzin cha dang //kha dog yig 'bru bsgyur byed rnams//de rnams phyogs su ma lhung na/ma gol gnas pa'i bsgom pa'o//bsgom pa'i mtshan nyid snang tshul ni//mtshan
52'b2(cha): nyid stong gsal rig pa la//rang bzhin gang du ma 'dres pa//gzung dang 'dzin pa'i mtha' zang dang //chas nyid 'dzin pa'i mtha' zang dang //chos nyid rang ngo zang dag pa'o//snang tshul la khrul pa ma yin par//rgyu rkyen dag pa'i rang bzhin lha/
52'b3(cha): dngos dang dngos med rang dag gnas//sgo lnga'i yul zhen dag pa dang //'byung ba'i rags pa dag pa dang //ye shes dag pa'i 'phel snang ngo //de la zad pa'i chos nyid do//ma bsgoms pa'i skyon nyi ni//'khor ba'i mtshan nyid/
52'b4(cha): mngon snang bas//bdag dang gzhan te yul shes dang //tshig dang bcas pa'i lta ba dang //dmigs dang bcas pa'i yul dag dang //nyon mongs bdag tu mching bar 'gyur//sangs rgyas lam yang stor bar 'gyur//'bras bu'i
52'b5(cha): rang bzhin ma shes dang //chos kun mnyam pa'i gzhi med dang //rang rig khams gsum bcings pa dang //rtog dang bcas pas ltung bar 'gyur//gzhan yang chos nyid 'khor lo bshad//gnas pa'i lta bas chos kun rtogs///
52'b6(cha): rang bzhin dag pas 'khor 'das phyed//byas pa med pas byung sa grol//yang dag lam du stong pas khyab//rdzogs 'byung chos nyid stong pa'o//bya ba med pas byung med rdzogs//'khor ba dag pas 'das pa'o//gzhan yang
52'b7(cha): mthong dag lta ba ni//dngos med rang dag rdzogs pa che//lha la mchod pa mi dgos te//sngags kyi las dang dka' thub med//bdug pa mar me lha bshos ci//tshogs dang bla ma mchod pa med//gtong dang sbyin bsreg mchod pa med/
53a1(cha): pha rol byin dug las mtha' med//de ltar gni rtsa gang med pas//srog gcong ma byin len dang mi tshangs spyod//lta ba med dang bsgom med spyod pa med//ltar med spyad du med pas bsgom pa'i 'dod pa gang /
53a2(cha): stong dang gsal bas sems kyi 'dod pa 'gags//chos dang sangs rgyas chos med pa'o//gzhan yang rang bzhin lta ba ni/sa chu me rlung 'byung ba mngon sangs rgyas//bcos pa med par rang grol chen po nyid//'dod chags zhe sdang
53a3(cha): gti mug byang chub gzhi//rnam rtog las kyi gcod pa'i rtog pa'i gdeng //'khor ba ma spangs sangs rgyas dgongs pa'o//lus dang ngag yid dag pas sku gsum rdzogs//sen ma rig 'khrul pa'i chos nyid bzhi//las dang bag chags
53a4(cha): chags zhen pa lta gan do//gzhan yang bcos pa'i lta ba ni//chos las ma byung sangs rgyas la//rgyu yis ma bskyed 'bras bu rdzogs//rkyen gyis mi 'jigs lus blangs te//lung las ma byung grub pa'i mtha'//rgyu la ma brten man ngag
53a5(cha): gis na mya ngan 'das pa'i rtsa ba bcad //'khor ba'i 'bras bu smin par byas//yang dag ma yin ma dag gzhi//nyon mongs drug gi gzhi ma la//byang chub sa bon stongs par byas//ngan song gsum gyi gzugs smin te//sku gsum po yang lag
53a6(cha): changaso//gzhan yang brjod bya'i lta ba bshad//srog ma bcad pa sang mi rgya/skar skung dag ni ma bkag na//mun pa dag ni sangs pa min/sangs rgyas rnams kyi slob dpon ni//dmyal ba chos kyi rgyal po la//chos kyi ngan song gsum du
53a7(cha): khri /srog bcad pas ni dge thob bo//bltas pas khams gsum 'khor ba la/bsgoms pas nyon mongs tshogs kun rdzogs//spyod pa spyad pas gol sgrib la//'bras bus 'khor ba'i rgyu nyid do//gzhan yang brda don tshig 'brel bshad/
53b1(cha): bskam pa'i mi dadung grong dkyil nas/mi cig cig pa med par yang //khams sde snod chos 'chang cing //las su gzhan gyi srog gcod pa//dge sbyor 'phel ba la re ma/lcags kyi khang pa sgo meng ma/snang med mun pa'i klong dkyil ma
53b2(cha): nyi ma zla ba 'od gsal yang //sus kyang mthong ba med pa ni/so so'i dbang po'i yul gsal ba/mthong ba med pa ya re ma//gzhan yul 'dzin pa lta bo bshad //khams gsum 'khor ba'i gzhi stongs pas//mya ngan 'das pa phyags mas
53b3(cha): btag sa ma rig 'khor ba'i rtsa bcad pas//sku gsum 'bras bu drung nas 'byin//mun pa'i gos gyon nyi ma snrubs//gsal ba'i mthongs phye mun pas btibs//rno ba'i ngar btags cig mchod//rgya che dkyil stong cig ma shong //sna tshogs
53b4(cha): 'dus pas cig ma rnyed//yod med mtha'i rtog bsal lo//gzhan yang 'dod sems lta ba bshad//kun rgyab gcig ma sod pa yi//cig rgyab thang song bar byas//mi brgya lcags sgrog gcig mthar//cig bzung pos ni thad thar//thar pas gang du 'gro
53b5(cha): lam zad//'jug pa las kyi rgyu 'gag so//gzhan yang spyod pa'i 'khor lo bshad//ma byung bas na bdag med cing //mi len mi 'dor sku'i rgya//byung ba mi brtsal yod mi spang //'dod pa bskyod cing chags mi bya//chags par bya ste btang bar bya'o//
53b6(cha): gzhan lus mi spang mtho mi 'tshem/sngags dang phyag rgya rgyun mi bcad //grub mtha' mi spang chos mi spyad//grogs dang bla ma mi smad do//gzhan yang dam tshig sdom pa bshad//bla ma sangs rgyas mchod pa'i rten//chos dang dge 'dun yi dam lha//
53b7(cha): rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma rnams la/chogs dang mchod brgyan gyi bcing /bla ma mtshan nyid ldan mthong na/bstod dang mchod pa rdzogs par bya//bud med mi smang khro mi bya/phung po mi smad yid dam bsten/dkyil 'khor dang ni sngags dang lha//phyag rgya bsten
54a1(cha): @pa yang dag nyid//dang can tshogs gnyis ma bcag la//dge ldan sems ni ma dkrugs te//sdom ldan dam tshig gnas par bya'o//gzhan yang bslab pa'i rim pa nyid//lus kyi spyod pa rdzogs bya ste//ngag ni rab tu bcad
54a2(cha): par bya//sems ni gnas dang brtag cing dpyad//rims 'jug las kyi rim pa'o//tshang dang mtshan nyid shes par bya//rtag tu byug dang ril bu yis//rtsa rnams thig le'i dra bar gzhug/phreng ba do shol phyed pas yang //mdzes shing 'kho/
54a3(cha): ba'i bya ba spel//las kyi bya ba dngos med gsal//dngos grub mchog gi gtso bos blang //gnas drug brgyan gyi 'khor los bya'o//gzhan yang brten cing 'jug pa nyid//bla ma bsten pas 'khor rgyun 'gag/dbang bskur bsten pas
54a4(cha): lus ngag smin/dam tshig sten pas dngos grub byung //lta ba brten pas khams gsum 'chang /bsgom brten pas ni 'khrul rgyun 'gag/spyod pa brten pas lus sbyongs 'gyur//brten bya 'di las gang byung ba/'di nyid dam tshig sdom pa'o//
54a5(cha): 'jug pa'i lam ni rnam pa brgyad//lus kyi 'jug pa lnga yi no//ngag ni rnam pa brgyad du 'gyur//sems kyi 'jug pa sum cu gnyis//lus sems khyad par byed pa nyid//de las ye shes khyad nyid do//gzhan yang bsgom pa'i gnas 'khor
54a6(cha): nyid/lus kyi dkyil 'khor sna tshogs kyis//sbyangs pas sems kyi gnas par 'gyur/gsum gyi sbyor bas rtsa rnams mnye//sems ni babs kyis dgug par bya'o//mtshan ma bzo'i sbyor ba yis//lus sems thabs kyi khyad par gyis//
54a7(cha): rnakhyor sems ni gnas par bya'o//gzhan yang 'khrul pa'i zhe rgyun bcad//nyi zla'i 'od sbubs rgya mtsho grangs stong gsum ri rab srang la bcal/ma rig gzhi zlog rang rig go//'khor ba'i spyi phyung 'das lam bkag/chos dang gang zag
54b1(cha): phyag mas btag/'bras bu rang rdzogs chos nyid gzhi//bya dang byed pa rang 'gag so//gzhan yang 'dod sred drungs nas 'don//chos kyi 'khrul pa yang gzhi byas//de nyid rang sar grol ba'i yul//khams gsum las byas sangs rgyas
54b2(cha): med/ ston pa'i bstan pa su la srid med pa'o//chos kun gzhi med rtsa ba su la srid //srid bral 'dod pa med pa'i sems//rangab bzhin ma bcos brjod las 'das//bya byed 'khrul pa'i bzlog pa zad //chos med sangs rgyas med cing son med//
54b3(cha): nga med bdag med bzung dang 'dzin pa med pa'o//gzhan yang chos ska ro cig bshad//tshig tu brjod pa ma yin te//sems zhes gnas pa tsam du'ang med//med phyir stong pa'i snang ba la/dran shes rnams kyi khungs zad pa'o//byas pa med pa'i chos
54b4(cha): nyid las//byung ba med pa ya mtshan che//'byung ba med pa'i chos sku la//'gro ba med pas ya mtshan che//'khor ba med pa'i rig pa la//byung ba med pa'i chos nyid do//gzhan yang 'dus shing mnyam par bshad//du ma spangs pas tha snyad bral//
54b5(cha): gam grub pas rang bzhin dag/rang bzhin gsang bas don grub pa'o//rang dag pa dang gzhi ma dang //'khor dang 'dren pas sangs rgas la//srog dang sring po med pa dang //ya ma bla zhes mtshon bralo//gzhan yang rang bzhin 'khrul ba bshad//dus
54b6(cha): dang dbang po nyid dang yang //bye brag 'jug pa so so yi las//phung po khams rnams skye mched do//rgyu dang rkyen pas skad cig la//blo bur sprin phung lta bu las//lus dang grib ma bzhin du 'khor//'khrul gzhi ltos pa tha dad las//seMn las ni sna
54b7(cha): tshog so//gzhan yang dag pa'i snang ba bshad//chos nyid mngon gsum snang ba ni//dbang po sgo la nges 'thon ste//sprin med pa'i mkha' la gsal//nyams kyi snang ba gong 'phel ni//ye shes kha dog phyir byung nas//gyen 'grengs pa dang rtsibs shar ba
56a1(cha): @thig le sna tshogs sku nyid du//rags par snang ba'i yul la gsal//rig pa tshang phebs snang ba ni//longs sku'i mtshan dper gsal ba ni//ma nges kha dog 'ja' tshon las//rigs lnga yab dang yum du snang /
56a2(cha): chos nyid zad pa'i snang ba nyid//nyams kyi snang ba stongs nas ni//lus zad dbang po'i yul zad kyang //rtog tshogs 'khrul pa las grol nas//brjod gzhi tshig dang bral ba'o//gzhan yang 'byung ba'i sgra rnams bshad//me'i sgra ni 'tsheg
56a3(cha): pa dang //stag dang bslab dang 'khro ba dang //rna myor ba yis don mi 'grub//bsre zhing 'bar ba'i bar med dang //dal ring rtse mo rno ba ni//rna khyor pas ni don chen 'grub//kha dog nyid kyang rnar bshad//dmar gdangs rtse mo gnag pa dang //dum bu rang
56a4(cha): gi dngos su 'chang //bar chod nyid ni 'byung ba'o//dkar gsal pad+ma lta bu la/sngo zhing kha dog sna tshogs la//'khor lo gdugs dang mtshon rtse dang //dmar gsal gdangs pa'i zer 'phro la//rdo rjer rgyal mtshan dril bu dang //mig mangs
56a5(cha): ris dang gur khyim dang //mchod rten brtsegs pa la sogs ni//rna khyor bas ni rdzogs pa che//don la mngon gsum byas nas ni//khams gsum dag las 'da' ba'o//gzhan yang 'byung ba'i yan lag ni//chu ni gshang dang sna tshogs sgra//dkar ba'i ri
56a6(cha): mo dung ma ston//rna lhor pas ni don rab 'grub//saM ni ljig la bsing ba dang //zur la 'bar zhing 'gril bas 'grub//rlung ni sprir zhing gzir ba dang //sngo zhing phyur ba dag gis so//de ltar rtags la ma sbyar na//cho ga bsgyur la mkhas pas brtsam//
56a7(cha): lan gsum lnga dang bdun gyis yang //'grub 'gyur 'di la the tshom med//so so'i las dang nges sbyar ba'o//ces smra tha'i 'gyur chen po//chos rnams kun gyi rtsal las//lha bsgom bkod pa gnas pa'i rtsa ba bstan pa'i le'u ste lnga pa'o//
56b1(cha): de nas chos rnams brjod med kyang //son 'khrul rtog du ma las//sgra tshig tha snyad gni mar yang //bcos pa med pa'i mamakha' las//tshig 'byung ston pa'i khyad par bzhugs//pad+ma las byung sangs rgyas la//dga' byed kyis ni 'di skad gsol//rtse
56b2(cha): pad+ma'i dbus na bzhugs pa nyid//'di ltar na mkha'i dkyil 'khor la//'od kyi thigs pa 'bum phrag ni//drug cu rtsa bzhi snang ba dang //yang zhun bcu bdun ci'i rgyu//chos kyi sku ni ji lta bu//longs sku zhes ni gang la bgyi//sprul pa'i sku ni gang tsam zhig/sku'i
56b3(cha): mtshan nyid ji lta bu//bzhugs pa'i tshul ni ji ltar lags//rang bzhin chos nyid sku rnams ni//bcom ldan 'das kyis bdag la gsungs//de nas bcom ldan rdo rje 'chang //pad+ma'i dbus nas bzhengs nas kyang //na mkha' lding gi phyag rgya mdzad//de nyid
56b4(cha): mdzad pa rtsam gyis ni//chos nyid rang snang na mkha' las//brjod du med pa'i rang bzhin las//brjod bzhi'i tshig tu 'di skad gsungs//rang byung byas pa med pa byung //kye kye lha'i dbang phyug nyon//'od dkar 'bum phrag drug cu ni//rtsa bzhi yang dag
56b5(cha): bshad bya'o//rang bzhin rdzogs pa chen po las//tshig gi bye brag rnam 'phros pas//sde gsum las ni glong dgur 'gyur//tshig bsdus pa las sho lo ka/'bum phrag drug cu rtsa bzhi las//le'u stong phrag sum cu lnga//bam po nyi khri
56b6(cha): stong phrag cig/brjod bya na mkha'i mtha' dang mnyam//gdul bya sen rnyog pa gcod//'di la brjod bya nyer sdus pa//gzer bu brgya dang brgya nu la//'gags ni spong phrag phyed dang gnyis//gnad chen 'dus pa stong phrag gsum//gom sgril
56b7(cha): khri zag bzhi bcu la//ming thogs rgyud ni khri phrag gnyis//brjod tshig rim pa gsal 'akod de//'od kyi yang zhun bcu bdun bshad //rang shang ba dang rang grol ba//rang byung nyid dang rtsal rdzogs dang //mdzes ldan dag dang 'dra bkong dang /
57a1(cha): mu tig 'phreng ba yi ge med//snying dang thugs dang me long dang //nyi zla kha sbyor ngo sprod spras//sku gdung 'bar dang rin chen spungs//sgron ma 'bar dang klong chen drug/de ltar bcu drug rim pa rnams//thal 'gyur las nI
57a2(cha): nges 'phros nas//gdul yul son rnams la snang //bcud 'dus bstan pa 'dzin pa ni//bskal pa yod pas 'khor las sgrol//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas mngon snang ba'o//chos kyi sku ni sku dang gsung //thugs dang yon 'phrin las so//
57a3(cha): sU ni stong dang gsal ba dang //mtshan ma med pa'i rang bzhi no//gsung ni sgra tshig ming 'bral nas//brjod cing gleng ba nyid dra'o//thugs ni bsam dang 'gyu med las//'phro zhing gcod pa rnams las 'das//ston phyir mi 'ong dang bzlog pa med/
57a4(cha): dbyings dang ye shes khyab pa dang //rang bzhin bcos pa med pa'o//'phrin las ma byung ma skyes byas pa med//shugs 'byung ba dang 'gag pa med//ma byas byas pa med pa'o//longs spyod sku yang sku gsung thugs//yon 'phrin las lnga
57a5(cha): yi no//sku ni snang ba rang bzhin med//gsal dang gsal byed rig pas khyab//gsung ni rang 'byung ba dang rang snang dang //spro bsdu rnam pa'i 'khor lo yis//thugs ni rgyu med gzigs pa dang //mkhyen pa'i rtsal dang dgongs pa'o//ston mtshan
57a6(cha): dpe rdzogs pa la//dgongs gsal mngon shes rdzogs pa'o//'phrin las chos kyi 'khor lo las//phyi nang gsang ba rnam spros te//so so'i rigs kyi sangs rgyas dang //byang chub sems dpa' rnams la yang //rang bzhin ljags la rnam
57a7(cha): spros te//'khor la rang gi ngo bo ston//sprul pa'i sku yi sku gsung thugs//yon 'phrin las lnga la'o//sku ni mtshan dang dpe byad la///gdul bya gang byung lus sprul la//gsung ni tshig sbyor legs pa la//tshangs pa'i dbyangs ni yan lag
57b1(cha): drug thugs ni shes bya'i ye shes kyis//rang bzhin don gnyis dgongs par gnas//ston mkhyen pa rdzogs pa dang //'phrin las phyi nang gsang ba yi//mdzad pa ma lus rdzogs pa dang //phun sum 'tshogs pa lnga gan so//sku'i mtshan
57b2(cha): nyid ngo bo dang //nges tshig byed pa rnams dang ni/so so'i zhing khams gdul bya dang //byed pa'i las dang ldang tshad //mtshan nyid go ba'i khyad kyis yang //mtshan nyid chos las grub pa dang //'dod yon rnams las grub pa dang //sna tshogs bkra/
57b3(cha): las grub pa'o//go ba rang bzhin dag pa dang //so so'i snang yul ston pa dang //gzugs dang dbang po rdzogs pa'o//sku'i bzhugs tshul rnam pa gsum//mtshan ma'i chos las grol ba'i phyir//rigs can gzugs su ma yin dang /
57b4(cha): gsal ba'i rang bzhin rdzogs pa'i phyir//snang la rang bzhin med pa dang //gdul bya'i rgyud rnams spyad pa'i phyir//mi g.yo mnyam gzhag chen por bzhugs//mi g.yo ba dang gsal ba dang //rig cing mkhyen pa'i char snang dang //mnyam
57b5(cha): dang phyogs med mi 'byed pa'o//sku'i rang bzhin chos nyid ni//rang bzhin stong dang gsal ba dang //mkhyen rig ma 'gags mngon snang ba'o//sku'i chos nyid rdzogs pa dang //phyir mi ldog dang 'gro 'ong med//smra bsam brjod pa med pa'o//
57b6(cha): gzhan yang sku rnams bsgrub pa ni//gong du bstan pa'i khyad par las//chos sku'i sa bon rang sems las//mngon gsum pa'i gnad gzir nas//rtag tu goms par byas pa yi//chos kyi sku'i bsgrub pa'o//longs sku'i sa bon
57b7(cha): 'dod yon la//bslabs te goms par byas pa yis//longs spyod rdzogs pa'i bsgrub pa dang /sbrul rgu'i sa bon 'byung ba la//sma'i don rnams bslabs nas ni//goms pas sbrul sku bsgrub po'o//bzhin yang me'i las mtha' ba shang/
58a1(cha): @#/kha dog gi ni rnam phye ba//gar gsal 'jam ma zhi ba 'grug/ser 'phreng ring na rgyas pa 'grub//dmar mdangs 'phro ba dbang gi las//sngo zhing nag thung drag po 'grug/sna tshogs 'chang cing 'khyil ba la/
58a2(cha): sna tshogs las kyi bye brag 'grub//shin tu 'phro na ltung bar 'gyur//'chad cing drag na bar chad 'byung //mched dka' me lce du bar ldan//dam tshig nyams par gyur pa'o//sgra yis rnam par phye ba ni//lhab lhab gsum
58a3(cha): bstud par par mang //sna tshogs las ni 'grub pa'o//phrag dang 'tsheg na drag po nyid/'ur zhing thug na zhi rgyas gnyis//gshang zhing sing na mchog 'grub bo//gzhan yang tshu'i las mtha' bshad//kha dog nges pa'i
58a4(cha): rtags nyid ni//dkar ba la sogs bzhi yis ni/zhi ba la sogs 'grub par nges//kham nag gtsug dang 'khyil bas nA//rnal 'byor pa yis don mi 'grub//dkar gsol 'phyar dang 'phyur bas na//mchog gi dngos

84b1(cha): nyid yul la phar ltas pas//sna tshogs bral ba'i rig pa nang du mthong //rang rig du ma'i yul la phar ltas pas//chos sku rtog med chen po rang la rnyed//sangs rgyas ye shes rgya chad ye nas med//long sku'i yis rgya me rnam par rol//bya dang byed pa med par lhun grub yin//rtogs pa rang snang chos nyid

84b7(cha): skor//'dzin rtog med pa'i rang byung bde chen ston//sangs rgyas bden pa mthong na rgyal ba'i 'bras//rtogs pa khong du chud na nams kyang bde//'byung ba grogasu shar na snang ba dag/yin min gnyi su shes na rtog pa'i tha bani grol//gcod byed yid ma 'gyus na sangs rgyas ngo rnyed/phyogs me/

99b2(cha): gsum la thug rdzogs chen snang ba thug phrad nyid//gzung bas zin par mi 'gyur te//rnam dag ngo lnga le thug/rdzogs chen rtog med gsal stong nyid//chags pas don dam mi rtog ste//yin pa rnam pa drug la thug/rdzogs chen rnam pa bzhi rdzogs nyid//spyad pas mtha' la mi
99b3(cha): stsol te//'dzin med rnam pa brgyad la thug rdzogs chen 'gyur med lam lnga nyid//sbyangs pas phyin par mi 'gyur te//dkyil 'khor rnam pa gsum la thug /rdzogs chen 'bras bu rang sangs nyid//bsrungs pas thug par mi 'gyur te//nyi shu rtsa gcig thabs lo thug rdzogs chen gas
99b4(cha): sngags de nyid//mchod pas dngos grub mi 'gyur te//zil non rnam pa gsum la thug/rdzogs chen stong nyid ye shes nyid//bsams pas rig pa mi mthong te//bsta ne ba nga 'i snying po gsum la thug/rdzogs chen rang gdangs 'od gsal nyid//sprang ba mthong bar mi 'gyur te//don
99b5(cha): gyi snang ba gsum la thug/des ni rdzogs chen mtha' la skyol//rang rig ston pa de bzhino//e ma ho//snang ba chen po gsang ba che//ye nas gnas pa'i gsang sngags bcud//rdzogs pa chen po'i ye shes yin//sangs rgyas bas pa kyang che ba nyid//rdzogs chen mthar thug ye nas don//kun gyi snying po ste
99b6(cha): pas na ru gsang sngags rnams kyi the tsoms//rnam dag ye shes 'od gsal bas//'byung ba'i ye shes 'odu gsal//chags pa med pa'i ye shes ni//rtogs dang ma rtogs gnyi su med//sangs rgyas nyid kyi snang ba ni//phyogs dang rigasu ga la yod//kun tu bzang po nga'i sems//dngos po med las rtog/
99b7(cha): mthar brag mtha' dang bus mi 'byed ma/rig pa'i ye shes snang ba nyid/sems dang bral ba'i rtag su bstam/langs pa'i grong khyer gting mthar las//gas la ba'i grong khyer thug pa med//rdzogs pa chen po'i ma yis mi/da ba dag dum yis na/sa gas snang brdzogs par 'gyur
100a1(cha): #mtha' la mi gnas rdo rje'i sku//rkyen gyis mi shigs stong nyid che//gsang ba'i rig pa kun tu khyab//e ma lta ba'i don la thug pa med//thug med ye shes ngo nyid//snang ba bzhi'i dri ma gsal//sangs rang ma sangs gnyis med kyang //ltar snang nyid la gnyisu byung //rdzogs chen
100a2(cha): b+ha ba'i zugs pags sa dang ma sangs gnyis dang thA la/rdzogs can k+Sha ba'i sgra la sa mi ya ci yang med pa la//sang na sna tshogs 'gyu ro/yas dang dog pa gnyis med/yin dang min pa gnyisu med/sa dpas 'das gnyis med/gas dang ma rtogs gni su med/
100a3(cha): ngan pa gnyi su med//mtha' dang dbu su gdag su med//gol dang sgribs pa gnyisu med//dge dang sdig pa gnyisu med//gsungs dang ma gsungs gnyi su med//de ni gnyis med lta ba'o//rdzogs chen rang gsal ngo la//mun dang snang ba med shes na//yang dag don gyi dngos po snyed//sngags dang byas/
100a4(cha): rgyab sgyur rdzogs/ting 'dzin yig 'brum sgoms gsal/snying po mchod pa ma byas grub//gzung 'dzin gnyi su med pas rdzogs//skyed rims ma byas lha tshogs mthong //goms pa cig gis pha rol phyin/ting 'dzin cig gis sa rnams nom//lta ba cig gis phyi nang mthong //sgom pa cig gis 'a
100a5(cha): med snyed//spyod pa cig gis rang snang rtogs//'bras bu cig gis 'dod pa grub//dper na khang stong mun nag nyid//snang ba cig gis sel ba ltar//ma rig 'khor ba'i mun pa rnams//snang ba cig gis thaMd sel//sangs rgyas kun gyi ye shes dang //son kun gyi rig pa gnyis//cig
100a6(cha): las med por mgon pos bshad//sangs rgyas snang ba dmigs med dang //son snang ba dmigs pa can//cig nyid yin par rgyas pas bshad//gsal ba'i dkyil 'khor 'od zer ni//mu khyud rab zlum por gnas//klu'i 'od snang 'di lta ste//gsal 'tsher lhun sdug gzu
100a7(cha): 'bar 'od zer kun tu 'phro ba'o//mdog bzang lhun yangs dkyil 'khor 'byung //sna tshogs sku las 'byung ba'o//ye khyab lhun rdal thaMd skyed//ye shes 'gag med 'char ba'o//ye shes dang ni yang dag sems//sku ni sna tshogs 'phro ba'o//rdzogs chen ye shes snang ba nyid//sna tshogasu ni/
100b1(cha): 'byung bar bshad/stong pa dang ni gsal ba'i sku/'byung ba dang ni ma 'byung med//shes pa dang mami shes med//mthar yas don la 'gyur ba med//mthar thugartags la zad pa med//mtshan ma'i tshig la thug pa med//rang rig don la 'gyur ba med//gsang sngags don la phyogs myur
100b2(cha): med son lus la stong pa med//sangs rgyas sku la skye 'bri med//ye shes don la 'gag pa med//'khrul snang nyid la zin pa med//'od zer nyid la chags pa med//rdzogs chen don la tha snyad med//rang rig sku la gzung 'dzin med//kye ma phyog su ltas pas grub pa med//rigs dang
100b3(cha): bral bas kha dog med//brjod gzhi med pas snang mtha yas//e ma dkyil yangs kun tu gsal//rang rdzogs ye shes snang du rung //rdzogs chen dus na rang snang ba//gsang sngags rtogs pa'i mchog tu bstan//dgongs pa rnam dag ye shes don/tha dad ma yin gcig pu nyid//gas sa
100b4(cha): rdzogs chen ye shes nyid//dper na 'dam gyi pad+ma bzhin//rig pa'i ye shes ngo nyid//mun pa nyid las snang bar 'gyur//snang ba nyid las mun pa byung //'khrul pa nyid las ye shes skye//dag snang nyid las 'khor bar ltung //phyogs cha nyid dang bral ba'o//sangs rgyas nyid las son ste/snyen

101a1(cha): #/bstan du gsol//ye shes rtags dang rab tu ldan/ming dang mtshan ma las grol ba'i//bangs chen yan lag bstan du gsol//ting 'dzin las rab tu bzhengs//de gang zhu ba phul ba dang /bcom ldan kun bzang se d+he de/ting 'dzin las bzhes nas ni/rigs kyi saspyas ran/

101a3(cha): du gnas//de yang gnas lugs 'di lta ste//rtsi ta rin chen gzhal yas dang //rnam dag dung khang gzhal yas dang //'bri ku rta'i gzhal ya so//gzhal yas chen po de rnams nas//rig pa'i ngo rtsal ltar snang //de yang bshad pa 'di lta ste//la la dag na sku ru gnas//la la dag la thig ge
101a4(cha): tirje gcig na 'od zer tshul du gnas//gcig nas lu gu rgyu du byung //de ni gnas pa'i mdo nyid bstan//sangs rgyas ye nas gnas pa'i don//sen kun la gnas pa de//rgyal ba nyid kyis rab tu bshad//rig dang ma rig snang ba nyid//bya byed gnyis kyi mtha' las 'das//yangs pa/
101a5(cha): nyid dang sku ltar 'phro//gsal ba nyid dang 'od ltar 'byung //stong pa nyid dang zer ltar 'byung //sangs rgyas dang ni sen gnyis//rig pa so ma yin ste//snang ba so nyid bstan//sku'i mtshan nyid 'di lta ste//'gyur ba med dang gsal ba dang //stong pa dang ni rtog bra'o//rig pa'i
101a6(cha): mtshan nyid 'di lta ste//phra ba nyid dang 'phro ba dang //lu gu rgyud dang 'gul ba'o//dbyings kyi mtshan nyid 'di lta ste//yangs dang lhun sdug ting mtha' yas//gsal dang dro dang bsil ba dang //yang zhing g.yo' ba'i bdag nyid 'dzin//khyab dang mu khyud ra ba'o//ye shes mtshan nyid 'di lta
101a7(cha): ste 'byung dang byung dang rang gsal dang //ye dang shes dang rtogs pa'o//gsal dang mtsher dang 'byams yas nyid//snang dang mun dang go 'byed pa//stong dang gsang ba ri ye shes mchog gsang sngags mtshan nyid dag du 'dod//e ma yis thabs la mkhas//kun mkhyen ye shes
101b1(cha): mdzod 'dzin pa ri//sangs rgyas kun gyi sprul pa'i sku//rdo rje seder gsang 'jod ston//ye shes sku dang snang mtha' yas//ye shes chags pa mi mnga' yang //sen blo'i snang ba rnams//gsal bar bya yir rgyud 'di bshad//e ma gsang sngags thugs rje me//'byung ba lnga'i dra ba nas//ye shre

101b7(cha): byung bang ni rig pa'i rgyal po la//yul rkyen dang dri ma med//e ma yangs pa'i snang ba kun tu 'bar//gsal ba'i ye shes thug pa med//don gyi ye shes gting mtha' yas//'thun pa'i ye shes rang snang bas//rtags pa'i ye shes mngon par snang //thabs nyid ma 'gags don nyid mas/
102a1(cha): mtshan ma med pa'i rig pa nyid//'bras bu bcud len shing lta bu//sna tshogs skyed pa'i ye shes sku//rang gnas yin par nyid kyis bshad//thug pa med par kun tu rtogs//khab len rdol lcags 'du ltar//shes rab chen pos don rtogs na//rig pa'i snang ba thad sdud//
102a2(cha): ma sku dang rig pa'i snang ba yang /rang byung nyid kyi cho 'phru'o//shes rab nyid kyis snang ba mthong //rig pa nyid la rig pa med//ci phyir sna tshogs mtshon pa'i phyir//chags pa'i ye shes chu bzhi no//snang ba'i ye shes me yin no//spyod ba'i ye shes rlung bzhino//gsang ba'i thabs ni yan lag/
102a3(cha): nyid//nyi shu rtsa gcig dag du bstan//ye nas se shes rang gsal bas//dag pa'i ye shes mtha' dbus med//'byung ba nyid la byung ba med//rig pa nyid la byungs yod//gsang sngags don gyi snying po la//tshig dang brjod pas don ma snyed//sgron ma'i ye shes spyan gyi mchog/snga

102a7(cha): gsang ba dang ni mngon sum pa//byad med gcig du de bzhin gshegs//sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam sbyor ba/rig pa'i ye shes rtog dka' ba//rab tu gsang ba 'di bshad//yang dag nyid las yang dag rnyed/dkyil 'khor nyid las dkyil 'khor 'byung //dgongs pa'i gnas lugs rgyas pa'o//kye ho yes lus kyi 'azungs
102b1(cha): snang ba bas kyang mun pa che//grub na mun pa snang bar 'gyur//'khor ba'i bag chags sbyang dka' ba//yang dag des don 'di thob ma/skye ba med pa thob par 'gyur//rig pa'i ye shes bag chags kun las 'das//bag chags dag gis ma sgrib pa//dper na sbrul skogs sgyur ba bzhin//skad cig rtsi ya

239b4(cha): pa/ ye shes rang la 'char bar 'gyur//gzhi'i chos nying rdzogs 'gyur nas//lam la 'bras bu thob par 'gyur//de yang ye shes lhun gnas pas//nges pa'i bden pa rab mthong ba//shes pa'i khyad kyis gang gnas pa//don ni gcig las ma g.yos kyang //rang snang
239b5(cha): dkyil 'khor lhun gyi grub//ye nas rang rig 'od gsal bas//rang snang 'ja' tshon rnam par 'phel//snang ba ye shes skur ldan pas//shes rab ye shes khong nas gsal//de nas snang dang gsal ba dang //rang bzhin kun tu khyab pa'o//gsal ba'i/
239b6(cha): yo zhes snang 'byung bas//'khor ba'i mun pa rnam par gsal//tshig las shes rab rang gsal bas//'bras bu dkyil 'khor nyag gcig gsal//theg pa gcig la don brjod pas//so so'i bzhed gzhung bsam mi khyab//a nu'i mos pa go 'phye
239b7(cha): phyir//rang rang 'dod du bstan pa'o//de yang theg chen gsang ba las//yang dag nges pa'i lung bstan pas//a ru man chad theg pa rnams//stsol sems blo'i chos su bstan//rang gi don la mtha' yas kyang //gzhi la gcig las med pa nges//theg
240a1(cha): pa theb pa zhes bya ba//gud na yod pa ma yin te//rig pa stobs dang ldan gyur na//theg pa zhes ni de la bya//rig pa stobs dang mi ldan na//sna tshogs theg pa bsam yas 'gyur//ye shes cig dang
240a2(cha): du bral bas//gsang ba'i ye shes tshig las 'das//'dzin pa'i phur pas thams cad bzung //zhe 'dod 'khor ba'i spu bsngal chos//bsam gyi mi khyab rgya cher snang //yul du snang ba'i chos sku dang //sems can mos pa'i mtshan sku gnyis//

240a4(cha): gtan snying po tshig las 'das//mngon par byang chub sku nyid gsal//ye shes cig chod gnyis snang bral/rdo 'dzin sku ron mi 'gyur//mtha' gnyis mi gnas rangs che//phyag rgya'i rdo 'gyur ba med//mi spyod dkyil 'khor las byung ba'i//

241a4(cha): tu gsal bas 'gyur med zhing //rang rang rtsi ta rin chenan/mtshon gang 'od 'bar bum pa'i tshul//nang na ye shes me 'od 'bar/gzhal yas ye shes du ma snang //sku'i jo bo re re las//gsung gi sprul pa grangs med zhing //thugs kyi dgongs pa 'ang/

241a7(cha): rang rig ye shes po bo la//'od gsal dkyil 'khor lhun gyi grub//ye shes lam la rab gnas shing //chos yul gyi snang ba las//sangs rgyas 'od mi 'gyur ba yis//dang po'i sangs rgyas 'od bsrungs la//'od snang don nyid rab tu zhus//'od bsrung chen pos bka' stsal pa/

241b6(cha): las 'das//chos dang chos men lta ba yang //mthun par snang pa rtsam du zad //don gyi ye shes lngar ldan ni//yangs pa'i grong khyer rdo rje mtha' yas zhing //dkyil dang pho brang nyid ni yangs pa'i bdag nyid 'dzin//phyogs dang ris

246a1(cha): dag bI rig ye shes skul rang rig byang chub mtshan ma dngos bral bas//rig pa'i ye shes ci yang ma yin na//snang ba'i blo'i the tshom rab gsal na//chos nyid dngos bral ting 'dzin thob par gsungs//chos sku rig pa'i ye

246a3(cha): bsal chos nyid 'od gsal bas//mtha' la mi gnas dbyings la spyod//ci yang ma yin snying po'i don//'gyur ba ma yin drang por gnas//stong pa ma yin 'od gsal zhing //gsal ba mtshan mar ka med de//dngos por 'dzin pa'i yul las 'das//yod//
246a4(cha): pa ma yin 'dzin chags bral//med pa ma yin snying po gsum//sems ma bzhi rdzogs rnam dag 'od re gsal//gsal ba zang thal lhun re bstug/lta ba rmad byung ngo mtshar che//'gag med rang shar dang re spro//rdzogs pa kun khyab gsal snang
246a5(cha): che//dmigs med bsam bral dkyil 'khor che//rdzogs chen man ngag go ba bde//sangs rgyas nyag gcig thig le che//mtshan ma dngos bral yangs pa che//skye med rang byung sku lnga dag/chos nyid klong yangs khod re snyoms//thig le
246a6(cha): stong pa nyams re dga'//rdo rje rgyas 'debs bde ba che//gsang ba'i chos nyid klong re yangs//stong pa'i sku lnga 'od re gsal//bya ba rdzogs pas dkyil 'khor che//dmig med bsam yas lhun grub pa'o//kye ma snying po rdo rje brjod las 'das//
246a7(cha): stong pa kun snang chos sku ni//tha snyad dri ma rab tu sangs//'thun snang chos sku rang sdang zhing //rig pa'i sku la 'gyur med pas//bya bas rdzogs pa'i 'od lnga yang /lhun gnas lhun 'byam chen por sdang //'od lnga'i rang gdangs ma 'gags pas//
246b1(cha): mtshan mar 'dzin pa rnam rtog rgyu spyod lam thabs mkhas 'dzin chags bral//rdzogs pa'i klong du kun 'dus shar//rtog med gsang ba'i chos kun rdzogs//yang dag chos nyid gcig med pas//sems can blo la thug pa med//
246b2(cha): rang byung ye shes stongs pas yangs//chos sku'i ye shes rdzogs sangs rgyas//rdzogs chen sgron ma 'od gsal bas//stong pa nyid kyi lam du 'gyur//sgron ma de nyid mngon rdzogs pas//gsang sngags lame la te ring med//rang gi rtsi
246b3(cha): ta'i dkyil 'khor gyi//thig le nyag gcig rang rdzogs pas//ye nas gnas pa'i rdzogs chen don//gsang don rdzogs pa rig pa'i klong //thig le stong pa'i sgron ma dang //rang byung dbyings kyi sgron ma gnyis//stong dang mi stong mtha' las 'das//rtsol
246b4(cha): ba med par sngon gnas pa'i//snang ba rdzogs phyir rang gnas zhing //chos nyid mi 'bral dbyibs las 'das//rig pa'i ye shes sku lnga ba//rang snang 'od kyi dkyil yangs nas//'dzin chags bral ba'i rang snang yul//chos kyi bsam par sangs
246b5(cha): nas//rdzong du rdo rje'i 'khar rtsigs pas//cis kyang mi shigs rdo rje 'dra//rdo rje'i mkha' la skye 'jigs med//stong pa dngos po'i yul dang thal//phyogs med rnam par dag pa na//don dam kun rdzob gnyis su med//don ni rang gi rig pa la//rtags ni mngon snang klu
246b6(cha): dang bcas//snying po don dam 'gyur ba med//gsal 'khor lhun bstug 'od gsal bas//bde ba chen po dngos nyid las//sa bon rdo rje'i rgyu rkyen bral//rdzogs pa'i don las mi 'da' zhing //thabs mkhas stong pa'i dkyil na gnas//rig pa'i ye shes dngos po yang //
246b7(cha): sems can kun la rab gnas pas//kun gyis mthong bar dka' ba'i don//da yis rab tu bshad par bya'o//e ma rig pa'i gnas lugs ni//rang rang rtsi ta'i dkyil na gnas//yab yum gnyis kyi cha 'dzin pa'i//yab yum gnyis kyi tshul du gnas//yab kyi/
247a1(cha): cha 'dzin thabs kyi sku//yum gyi cha 'dzin shes rab sku//'od dang 'od zer tshul du gnas//dbyings dang rig pas rab brgyan ste//lhun rdzogs thig le chen por gnas//lta ba'i dngos po 'gyur med sku ltar gnas//sgom
247a2(cha): ba'i dngos po rtog med 'od ltar gnas//spyod pa'i dngos po stong dang gsal bar gnas//'bras bu'i dngos po gzhi rdzogs 'od gsal ma nyi ma//'dzin chags bral ba'i rdzogs chen gsang sngags che//de ni zhi ba'i bzhugs tshul lo//thig le bzhugs tshul bstam
247a3(cha): pa 'di lta ste//rtsa rnams nang na bI le'i sku ltar gnas//che ba na mkha'i mtha'i dang mnyam//chung ba'i tshad ni rta lnga bcur bshags tsama?//gsal ba'i tshad ni nyi zla'i dkyil 'khor 'dra//de yang gsal 'tsher gzi brjid ldan//'gro dang 'ong dang gnas pa'o///
247a4(cha): bya ba med pa'i 'gro 'ong can//rdzogs pa gzhi la rang rdzogs la//lam la thig le mngon par snang //de'i lta ba gcig gi ngang du rnam par rol//sgom pa nyi zla zer ltar 'phro//spyod pa rnam dag 'od gsal ba'o//rang rang mgo las khro bor gnas//yab dang yum
247a5(cha): du mngon par snang //khro dang 'khro ba'i tshul 'chang zhing //ye shes lnga lnga dag dang ldan//sku'i ye shes re re la//dbu'i ye shes lnga lnga'o//lta ba 'gag med zang thal lo//sgom pa rtog med mnyam par gnas//spyod pa 'gag med rol par 'char//'bras bu
247a6(cha): dbyings pa rig par ldan//de rab so la yang dag 'thod pa'i tshe//dbyings kyi rang bzhin shes rab sku//rig pa'i rang bzhin thabs kyi nu//'du 'bral med par sngon snang ba'o//sa 'gags 'gag med snying po che//brjid pa'i sku ltar mi gnas
247a7(cha): te/ngo bo rnam dag ye shes brdal ba nyid//stong pa chen po'i chos nyid yul la rol//gsal ba dang thal chos sku ngo bos stong //stong pa'i dus na rang ba'in ma nges te//sku dang ye shes dkyil 'khor rtogs pa med//thabs dang shes rab don/
247b1(cha): la 'gyur ba med//stong pa'i sgron ma bya rtsal gnyis dang bral//thugs rje 'byung ba sgron ma nyi zer bzhin//sgron ma'i 'od zer bting la thug pa med//gsang ba'i don ni rtsi ta'i nang du gsal//rang bzhin 'od ni nam mkha'i mthong na gsal//dkyil
247b2(cha): /'khor rang rdzogs 'od ni de bzhin no///ces nor bu 'phra bkod chen po'i rgyud las/snang ba'i ye shes ngos 'dzin pa'i le'u ste/bzhi pa'o//za/grA A ba paH/de nas lha mo dbyangs mkhan ma zhes bya bas/'di skad ces/
247b3(cha): gsol to//kye kye bcom ldan rdo rje sems//dmigs pa med pa'i phyogs dag nas//chos nyid rang snang don brjod pa'i//bdag cag rnam la gsung du gsol//de nas bcom ldan rdo rje sems dpa' yis//mos pa'i 'khor la bka'
247b4(cha): stsal pa//kye ma mos pa'i 'khor rnams nyon//nga yis rang rig chos bstan gyis//khyed kyis rtog med yid kyis nyon//sems kyi sdug bsngal ji snyed pa//skal ba'i bar du thar pa med//de nyid spang bar bya ba'i phyir//sangs rgyas 'od dpag med pa yis///
247b5(cha): don dam rig pa'i klong yangs nas//brjod med tshig bstan po mtshar tshe//nga yi sku la 'gyur med kyang //nga'i snang ba phyogs bcur snang //nga'i chos nyid rang snang yang //mun pa'i phyogs kyis yongs mi rlag/sems can snang ba bsam mi khyab//rnam drug ldan pa'i yul dag nas dad
247b6(cha): rkyen lnga rnam par 'phros pa yis sems kyi grogs ni bzhi dag las//blo'i snang ba drug du shar//rnam pa'i yul lngar rab tu bzung //bcu drug gam ni gsum dag gis//dri ma stong kha yas pa 'am//yang na stong phrag brgyad cu'o//snang ba'i dri ma gnyis dag las//ngar 'dzin
247b7(cha): dri ma gnyis snang/chos kyi dri ma bzhi dag las//rnam pa'i dri ma lngar snang ba'o//'thun pa'i sun pa gsum 'gyur ro//snang ba las ni mun pa 'byung //sun las snang pa nyid kyang bstan//blo'i spyod pa drug nyid las//rkyen ni dgu'am brgyad du brtan/
248a1(cha): dang po'i 'byung bzhi gcig pa la//de nas rnam pa bani dag las//'dra ba'i bani las golas gnyis//de rnam gsum 'am lnga dag byung /mun pa'i chos nyid bsam yas te//stong nga'i mun pa lnga nyid kyang /zhi zer sbyin sgrib rdzi bzhin
248a2(cha): dngos po'i mun pa drug rnams ni//dper na mig mdzes rol mo bzhin//de nyid la yang rab tu chags//'khor nang lam ni lnga nyid do//e ma'o de yang mun pa'i spyod pa rnams//nga'i snang ba 'di bstan pas//thams cad skye med mig dbene//gol sar skang /
248a3(cha): ba blo yin ste//blo'i snang ba bsam mi khyab//snang ba'i ye shes sku 'bar ba//rim pa'i spyod pa ji snyed pa//snang ba rnam pa lnga dag gis//mun pa'i phyogs dag sel bar 'gyur//rdzogs pa'i lta ba rnam drug gis//dri ma'i phyogs dag sel bar 'gyur//
248a4(cha): dkyil yangs dbyings ldan rnam gnyis kyis//gol sa rnam gsum sel bar 'gyur//rang byung bzhig thabs rnam gsum gyis//sgrib pa'i phyogs rnams sel bar 'gyur//rtogs pa'i rgyal po rnam dgu yis//rkyen lnga'i phyogs nas sel bar 'gyur//

248a6(cha): rang gi ngo bo ni//gnas lugs rnam pa gsum la thug/thugs kyi dgongs pa rtse gcig kyang //'byung sa chen po gcig la thug/don gyi ye shes chen po yang //bskyal sa chen po gnyis la thug/dri med rang gnang chos sku yang //shar

248b7(cha): sangs rgyas kun gyi gdung bzung ba//skye med snang ba'i rang lus thob//kyem gsang sngags nyid kyi lta ba'i gnas lugs las Da/rang bzhin nyid kyi snang ba rang snang las//rdo rje sems dpa'i me long 'di bstan pas//ye shes can gyis 'gro ba 'dren par 'gyur/sems
